Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any geochemistry module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 845. The projective resolution has length at most 4 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the geochemistry decomposition. By minimality, the element a^845 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 4-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
